SELECT [u1].[UserId], [u1].[BranchId], [u1].[CreatedOn], [u1].[DesignationId], [u1].[Email], [u1].[EmployeeId], [u1].[IsActive], [u1].[ModifiedOn], [u1].[Password], [u1].[PhoneNumber], [u1].[UserName], [s].[UserRoleId], [s].[RoleId], [s].[UserId], [s].[RoleId0], [s].[RoleName]
FROM (
    SELECT TOP(1) [u].[UserId], [u].[BranchId], [u].[CreatedOn], [u].[DesignationId], [u].[Email], [u].[EmployeeId], [u].[IsActive], [u].[ModifiedOn], [u].[Password], [u].[PhoneNumber], [u].[UserName]

) AS [u1]

FROM (
    SELECT TOP(1) [u].[UserId], [u].[BranchId], [u].[CreatedOn], [u].[DesignationId], [u].[Email], [u].[EmployeeId], [u].[IsActive], [u].[ModifiedOn], [u].[Password], [u].[PhoneNumber], [u].[UserName]
    FROM [Users] AS [u]
    WHERE [u].[EmployeeId] = @employeeId
) AS [u1]
LEFT JOIN (
    SELECT [u0].[UserRoleId], [u0].[RoleId], [u0].[UserId], [r].[RoleId] AS [RoleId0], [r].[RoleName]

    SELECT TOP(1) [u].[UserId], [u].[BranchId], [u].[CreatedOn], [u].[DesignationId], [u].[Email], [u].[EmployeeId], [u].[IsActive], [u].[ModifiedOn], [u].[Password], [u].[PhoneNumber], [u].[UserName]

) AS [u1]
